- [Geospatial Data - BI Connector - MongoDB Docs](https://www.mongodb.com/docs/bi-connector/current/schema/geospatial-data/): Understand how BI Connector maps geospatial indexes into numeric longitude-latitude arrays in MongoDB collections.
- [Load a Schema from a DRDL File - BI Connector - MongoDB Docs](https://www.mongodb.com/docs/bi-connector/current/schema/load-schema-from-drdl/): Load and edit a schema from a DRDL file for use with BI Connector, and apply changes by restarting `mongosqld`.
- [Auto Schema Mode (Persist a Schema in MongoDB) - BI Connector - MongoDB Docs](https://www.mongodb.com/docs/bi-connector/current/schema/persist-schema/): Persist a schema in MongoDB using `mongosqld` with options for schema storage, automatic resampling, and user permissions.
- [Resample Schema Data with "FLUSH SAMPLE" - BI Connector - MongoDB Docs](https://www.mongodb.com/docs/bi-connector/current/schema/resample-schema/): Regenerate the schema used by BI Connector with the `FLUSH SAMPLE` command or by restarting `mongosqld` to accommodate significant data shape changes.
- [Schema Management Changes in 2.11 - BI Connector - MongoDB Docs](https://www.mongodb.com/docs/bi-connector/current/schema/schema-management-changes/): Update your BI Connector configuration to version 2.11 for improved schema management, with changes in standalone, custom, and auto modes.
- [Sampling Type Conflicts - BI Connector - MongoDB Docs](https://www.mongodb.com/docs/bi-connector/current/schema/type-conflicts/): Resolve type conflicts in MongoDB fields using BI Connector by sampling data and generating relational schemas for scalar and composite types.
- [Use MongoDB Views - BI Connector - MongoDB Docs](https://www.mongodb.com/docs/bi-connector/current/schema/use-views/): Use views to control data visibility for BI Connector, allowing field selection, renaming, sorting, and transformations without a .drdl file.
- [Map Relational Schemas to MongoDB - BI Connector - MongoDB Docs](https://www.mongodb.com/docs/bi-connector/current/schema-configuration/): Map relational schemas to MongoDB using BI Connector, which provides methods for creating and managing relational schemas for business intelligence tools.
- [Supported SQL Functions and Operators - BI Connector - MongoDB Docs](https://www.mongodb.com/docs/bi-connector/current/supported-operations/): Explore the supported SQL functions, operators, and statements in the MongoDB Connector for BI, including comparison, logical, and string functions.
- [Create a System DSN - BI Connector - MongoDB Docs](https://www.mongodb.com/docs/bi-connector/current/tutorial/create-system-dsn/): Create a system DSN for the BI Connector's `mongosqld` process to configure SQL clients and BI tools for MongoDB data import.
